    SELECT TOP(1) [u].[Id], [u].[AccountNumber], [u].[CountyId], [u].[CreatedBy], [u].[CreatedDate], [u].[DOB], [u].[DeviceId], [u].[FailedLoginAttempt], [u].[FcmToken], [u].[FirstName], [u].[Gender], [u].[HireDate], [u].[Image], [u].[IsLoggedIn], [u].[KYCDocumentImage], [u].[KYCLevel], [u].[KYCStatusId], [u].[LastName], [u].[Permissions], [u].[PhoneNumber], [u].[SecurityAnswerText], [u].[SecurityQuestionId], [u].[Status], [u].[UpdatedDate], [u].[UserId], [u].[VerificationCode], [u].[VerificationExpiry], [u].[WleePhoneNumber], [k].[Id] AS [Id0], [k].[AccountBalanceLimit], [k].[Description], [k].[Name], [k].[TransactionDayLimit], [k].[TransactionMonthLimit], [k0].[Id] AS [Id1], [k0].[Name] AS [Name0], [c].[Id] AS [Id2], [c].[Name] AS [Name1], [a].[Id] AS [Id3], [a].[AccessFailedCount], [a].[ConcurrencyStamp], [a].[Email], [a].[EmailConfirmed], [a].[LockoutEnabled], [a].[LockoutEnd], [a].[NormalizedEmail], [a].[NormalizedUserName], [a].[PasswordHash], [a].[PhoneNumberConfirmed], [a].[SecurityStamp], [a].[TwoFactorEnabled], [a].[UserName]
    FROM [UserDetail] AS [u]
    LEFT JOIN [KYCLevel] AS [k] ON [u].[KYCLevel] = [k].[Id]
    INNER JOIN [KYCStatus] AS [k0] ON [u].[KYCStatusId] = [k0].[Id]
    INNER JOIN [County] AS [c] ON [u].[CountyId] = [c].[Id]
    INNER JOIN [AspNetUsers] AS [a] ON [u].[UserId] = [a].[Id]
    WHERE [u].[UserId] = @__userId_0
) AS [t]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [u0].[Id], [u0].[CreatedDate], [u0].[StaffRoleId], [u0].[UpdatedDate], [u0].[UserId], [s].[Id] AS [Id0], [s].[Name]
    FROM [UsersStaffRole] AS [u0]
    INNER JOIN [StaffRoles] AS [s] ON [u0].[StaffRoleId] = [s].[Id]
) AS [t0] ON [t].[Id3] = [t0].[UserId]
ORDER BY [t].[Id], [t].[Id0], [t].[Id1], [t].[Id2], [t].[Id3], [t0].[Id], [t0].[Id0]
